# P121 · PYVIBE-012 · OtoDock/oto-dock

- Repository at the pinned commit (may be consulted freely): https://github.com/OtoDock/oto-dock/tree/709a95ed50579cc22305821179f9afb179630614
- Location: [`satellite/_vendored/app_server_client.py:172`](https://github.com/OtoDock/oto-dock/blob/709a95ed50579cc22305821179f9afb179630614/satellite/_vendored/app_server_client.py#L172) · path class: main
- Rule: `PYVIBE-012` (python-vibe-guard), severity critical
- Message: asyncio.create_task() return value discarded — task may be GC'd and silently cancelled

## Enclosing function `start` (lines 138–181)

```python
 138      async def start(self, init_params: dict) -> dict:
 139          """Spawn the daemon, start the reader, run the ``initialize`` handshake.
 140  
 141          Returns the ``initialize`` result. Raises on spawn / handshake failure.
 142          """
 143          if self._started:
 144              raise RuntimeError(f"{self._label}: already started")
 145  
 146          cmd = [*self._sandbox_cmd_prefix, self._codex_bin, "app-server"]
 147          logger.info(f"{self._label}: spawning {' '.join(cmd[:4])}... (cwd={self._cwd})")
 148  
 149          # 200 MB stdout buffer — matches the CLI/Codex subprocess. Default 64 KB
 150          # readline raises LimitOverrunError on large MCP-result lines and kills
 151          # the reader mid-turn (see core/layers/cli/session.py).
 152          limit = 200 * 1024 * 1024
 153          if self._spawn is not None:
 154              self.proc = await self._spawn(cmd, cwd=self._cwd, env=self._env, limit=limit)
 155          else:
 156              self.proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
 157                  *cmd,
 158                  stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
 159                  stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
 160                  stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
 161                  cwd=self._cwd,
 162                  env=self._env,
 163                  start_new_session=True,
 164                  limit=limit,
 165              )
 166          self._started = True
 167          logger.info(f"{self._label}: daemon pid={self.proc.pid}")
 168  
 169          self._reader_task = asyncio.create_task(
 170              self._reader_loop(), name=f"{self._label}-reader",
 171          )
>172          asyncio.create_task(
 173              self._drain_stderr(), name=f"{self._label}-stderr",
 174          )
 175  
 176          result = await self.request("initialize", init_params)
 177          logger.info(
 178              f"{self._label}: initialized "
 179              f"(codexHome={result.get('codexHome')}, os={result.get('platformOs')})"
 180          )
 181          return result
```
